# CHANGELOG - CodexTactics UE port (moved from the old HANDOFF.md §10)

> Newest first. Every commit that adds / changes a system appends a row here (it used to be HANDOFF.md §10), and updates the matching `docs/systems/*.md`,
> `docs/port/PORT_MATRIX.md` and the `docs/port/TANDEM.md` log. Index: [HANDOFF](HANDOFF.md) | [system docs](../systems/README.md)

Old HANDOFF header (superseded by the new index, kept for history):

**Purpose.** Any agent (Claude, Gemini, …) must be able to pick up the port from this file alone.
Keep it current: every commit that adds / changes a system updates §5 (system map), §8 (next steps) and §10 (log).

Last update: 2026-09-29 by Claude, after commit `2ca14b0`.

## 10. Change log (newest first)

| Commit | What |
| (this, combat/suppression) | **feat(combat): suppressive fire (game designer decisions 2026-10-26, `docs/design/SUPPRESSION.md`).** Pure `Combat/SuppressionRules` (impact = hit / strike on his cover / near miss, pressure by weapon class x the weapon's `suppression_weight`, decay always + faster when quiet, tiers Suppressed 0.5 / Pinned 0.85 with hysteresis, explosion bursts, fire cells / fear zones, the Shift + click target pick, `IgnoresSuppression(bRage)`, the order's cadence / stop) + `USuppressionSubsystem` (level per unit, fed by `ShootAtTarget`, the new shared `AOperativeCharacter::FireAreaRound` (quick fire refactored onto it), the marksman, spitters / cryo drones, `ATurretActor` and `UVehicleTurretComponent` MG / cannon - the 2026-10-12 "no suppression stagger" note reversed - and every blast through `GameplayEvents::RaiseExplosion`). Effects: a suppressed / pinned RANGED enemy does not fire and holds his spot (marksman crouches / prone); a suppressed operative fires blind (hit x0.35, spread x3), no peek / corner hold / open shot; PINNED = no fire (orders wait); rage ignores it; Commander Mode's cover decision reads the level. Melee enemies are not suppressed: the squad's DENSE fire into an area opens a temporary fear zone (the burning-barrel fear, `AEnemyCharacter::FindNearestFireZone`) that fire-fearing packs flee / split round ("SCATTERS!"). Explosions suppress briefly (a fast-decaying burst). The SUPPRESS order: **Shift + click on enemies** (an enemy, a silhouette, a point within 4 m of a known enemy; LMB or RMB) with a RED marker (`UDefenseMarkerSubsystem`, the hold-line marker in red); Shift + click on it ceases it; elsewhere Shift + click keeps watch sector / hold line (green); no own key, no action-bar button; full-auto bursts with an assault rifle (`UWeaponDataAsset::FullAutoInterval`, weapons_tuning.json `full_auto_interval`, M16 0.1 s), ends on target down / ammo low (keeps 10) / any move, cover or attack order / turn-based; planned in the tactical pause. HUD "SUPPRESSED" / "PINNED" badges with a level bar. Turn-based: every level cleared, no round counts, orders end. A/B `Codex.Combat.Suppression` (default 1). Tuning `Content/Data/Combat/suppression.json` (staged UFS). Enemy-AI hook `SetSuppressIntent`. Tests `CodexTactics.Combat.Suppression.*` (11); smoke `SuppressionSmoke` (test_map rule `suppression-2026-10-26`). |
| (this, formation/runtime-v23) | **feat(formation): rules v2.3 in the runtime - walls into the trail column, ClearLook's held look, the narrow column's decided looks; 13.5 stand-ins keep their distance from every walkable slot; HUD "(turn)" for the turn fold (behind `Codex.Formation.Sectors` + `Codex.Formation.V21`).** FormationLabSmoke R_v2_gaps failed after 61990b7 ("the rear man keeps up - p95 211 cm"). Root cause (Debug 2 logs of every stand-in): the echelon right walks 3.6 m from the lab floor's edge, Z's slot is off the navmesh; v2.2 gave him the nearest stand-in - 50 cm from P's slot (Z on P's spot) - and the smoke measured the RAW slot ~2.2 m behind it, hiding that Z walked ~4 m forward BACKWARDS at the 0.9 x walk cap (389 -> 246 cm off the slot he was given). The 13.5 port counted only the slots resolved before him: Z after P -> the back-diagonal stand-in, raw-slot measure 212 cm (Z 10 cm off his stand-in); Z before P -> P's spot again, 285 cm against his given slot. Fix (runtime, contract 13.5 "and from the walkable slots"): `SeedFrameGivenSlots` gives out every walkable frame slot first; the smoke measures the GIVEN slot (`GetFormationGivenSlot`) as checks.ts 6 does; gate kept 150 cm (p95 13 cm). Wired (13.6): `FormationGuardRuntime::TrailFrameSlots` (TrailSlots with the world walls), `ClearHeld` + `AgeClearHeld` / `HeldClearLook` / `RememberClearLook` (reset whenever ClearLook does not apply or the look is not computed), `DecidedFixedLooks` -> the allocator's fixed looks (1A). HUD `Echelon right -> Column (turn)` (`GuardHudText(..., bTurnFold)`, `IsTurnFold`). `UEnvironmentSubsystem`: any `CodexTactics.` command in -ExecCmds is a dev check (smoke_parallel -PreCmds runs started on a random weather - a blizzard formation). `Codex.Formation.Debug 2` logs fallback stand-ins, 3 the held look. Tests `CodexTactics.Formation.V23Runtime.*` (6); 875 / 0 failed; FullUnity + Shipping; FormationLabSmoke alone x2 PASS, 22 smokes -Jobs 2 PASS, CVar 0 x4 PASS. |
| (this, formation/cpp-rules) | **feat(formation): rules v2.3 parts 1-5 port - the red-team fixes (FORMATION_CONTRACT.md 13, TS df3b3da).** `FormationSectorRules::ClearLook` gains the minimum free arc (`look.min_free_deg`), the held look (`look.hold_deg` / `look.hold_margin_deg`, new trailing `Held`), the narrow tier (`look.narrow_penalty`), the counted sweeps and the longest clear ray when enclosed; new `FormationSectorRules::Decided` (the narrow column's K / P / L looks stay fixed for the allocator); `FormationShapeRules::TrailSlots` gains trailing `Walls` (the stagger shrinks to keep `column.stagger_clear`). Config 74 keys. Runtime (13.5): `SquadFormation::ChooseFallbackSlot` + `USquadSubsystem::FrameGivenSlots` - two unwalkable slots never share a stand-in (`slot.min_spacing`). Tests `CodexTactics.Formation.V23.*`, `Formation.FallbackSlotsKeepSpacing`, `Parity.Decided`; 20 parity files 14356 cases, 0 mismatched / 0 skipped / 0 warnings; Shipping builds. |
| (this, formation/runtime-v21) | **fix(formation): a member made the leader looks forward, not his follower look (behind `Codex.Formation.Sectors`; the game bug behind TutorialSmoke's sight failure, confirmed by the coordinator: the leader's sector is 12).** `USquadSubsystem::SetLeader` remembers the old leader's own facing and starts the new leader's formation heading (= his halt look for 1 s) from `FormationRuntime::NewLeaderLookYaw`: walking his travel, an own order pending its direction, standing his own facing from when he last led (within 10 s), else the squad's heading; the switch is no teleport of the heading. Before, the heading was taken from his body - the rear guard's 6 o'clock he had turned to as a follower for 0.3 s - and he kept it as the halted leader. `Codex.Formation.Sectors 0`: the old path. Test `CodexTactics.Formation.Runtime.NewLeaderLook`; `SquadControlSmoke` switches the leader away and back (forward again within 0.5 s). |
| (this, formation/runtime-v21) | **fix(smokes): TutorialSmoke's "standing: the enemy is seen over the wall" with rules v2.1 - the sight scene is laid out along a CLEAR bearing.** The coordinator found it failing alone with `Codex.Formation.V21 1` (passing with 0); reproduced here in ~1 run of 3. Root cause (logged evidence, `Codex.Formation.Debug 3` + the new failure diagnostics): not the 1.2 m wall (bounds 120 cm, ignored by the v2.1 walls), not ClearLook / the corner watch / the guard - the standing Commander faced the enemy (sight facing = bearing to it, eye 162 cm) but the line was blocked 5.1 m on by `InteractableActor_1` (a 6 x 3.2 x 2.4 m container box). The scene (wall 1 m, enemy 7 m) is laid out straight along the leader's facing, and that facing is incidental: in the medkit lesson the leader is switched away and back for ~0.3 s, as a follower he turns to his place's look (the rear guard's) and keeps it as the halted leader; v2.1's different A3 / A4 walk left him at the overlook facing ~115 deg - into the container (v2.2 / `V21 0` left him elsewhere). Fix: the smoke tries `SightRules::BearingsNearestFirst` (new, test `CodexTactics.Combat.Sight.BearingsNearestFirst`) from the leader's facing in 20 deg steps and takes the first with a clear standing eye line on the sight channel (pawns see-through) and a free wall spot (trigger volumes ignored), turns the leader to it; on a failure it now logs who could see the enemy and what blocks each line. `Codex.Formation.Debug 3`: each member's formation look on change and every look ClearLook turns. |
| (this, formation/runtime-v21) | **feat(formation): rules v2.1 bypass "B" in the runtime (behind `Codex.Formation.Sectors` + `Codex.Formation.V21`; user decision 2026-10-25, FORMATION_CONTRACT.md 10.2).** A new own order of the leader in group mode whose straight way a FREE-STANDING wall blocks (`FirstBlockingWall`, agent radius 35 cm; room / corridor walls stay the nav mesh's): `FormationBypassRules::ChooseSide` with the threats the squad knows - reported (`ReportFormationThreat`) and its own sight / hearing (`UTacticalSightSubsystem::GetSquadKnownEnemies`: the enemies shown now, the last-known silhouettes with their age; with the squad's sight off, a clear line of sight) - the shortest side, or the shielded one when at most 30 % longer; the leader's order is steered through the chosen corners (`AOperativeCharacter::SteerOwnMoveVia` / `ResumeOwnMove`: the order, its destination and serial stay his; his next order drops it; not under a tutorial confinement). The guard's path ahead includes the remaining waypoints. New smoke `FormationBypassSmoke` (L_FormationLab lane G: R_G_free / R_G_threat_right -> the shortest side, R_G_threat_left -> the shielded side, R_G_far -> too long, and R_G_threat_left again with a parked hound the squad SEES; the leader's real side by `WallSideOf` on his path, no split); with the rules off it reports the nav mesh's side (the two threat-left runs pass the exposed side). Docs: squad-and-orders.md "Rules v2.1 in the runtime", FORMATION_SECTORS.md status, HANDOFF. |
| (this, formation/runtime-v21) | **feat(formation): rules v2.1 in the runtime - the wall guard, the trail column, the sharp-turn fold, the corner watch, ClearLook (behind `Codex.Formation.Sectors` + `Codex.Formation.V21`; user decisions 2026-10-25, FORMATION_CONTRACT.md 10).** The squad never splits round a wall: per frame `FormationGuardRules::Resolve` on the REQUESTED formation (the hold-G menu's choice - `USquadSubsystem::SetFormationMenuChoice`, dev console `Codex.Formation.Menu` - else the terrain's), checked at the leader and 5 m further along his nav path; a cut echelon takes its mirror, anything else the TRAIL column (`FormationShapeRules::TrailSlots` on the leader's recorded path; its sectors by each man's room); a sharp turn ahead (`TurnAhead` > 25 deg) folds an echelon / line into it; the request comes back after 5 m whole. | (this, agents/work) | **fix(gameflow,interactables,camera,ai,tactics,squad): the game designer's / user's Demo 01 play-test of 2026-10-22, part 2 - the squad stays where the fight ended, a moved barricade is vaulted both ways, the horde fly-in frames the wave, no heat fear through walls, the turn-based grid only on open level ground, operatives turn back to the enemy after a retreat.** (1) **Fight over -> the squad stays put.** Cause: `UWaveVictorySubsystem::StartPostCombatSequence` teleported every operative back to his preparation spot (Godot `_return_squad_to_prep_stations`; after the last wave / an ambush fight that is the whole battle) and the exploration formation then walked the followers to their slots. Fix (deviation from Godot, designer decision): no teleport (stations still recorded for saves), orders dropped, the commander still becomes the leader (Godot, kept - only the selection changes); `USquadSubsystem` arms a post-fight hold on PostCombat / a skirmish back to Exploration (`SquadOrderRules::ArmsPostFightHold`): the followers keep their spots, stance and cover until the leader moves (`KeepsPostFightHold`). Skirmishes never teleported. (2) **Moved barricade vaulted one way only.** Cause: `URelocationSubsystem::SetObjectCarried(false)` set the box `SetCanEverAffectNavigation(true)` when the barricade was put down, although `VaultNavigation::MakeVaultable` had taken it off the navmesh: the moved barricade cut a hole into the navmesh, the paths across were gone (walk round) and only a vault the leader started by himself crossed it. Fix: a vault-made object keeps its shapes off the navmesh, its vault area (`VaultNavigation::FindVaultNavModifier`) is refreshed at the new spot. (3) **Horde fly-in showed nothing.** Causes: the intro filmed EVERY registered enemy (the lake's patrols / parked zombies far away pulled the mass centre and the type shots off the horde), the wide distance was a span heuristic (too far for a tight pack, not enough for a spread one), the trigger could fire before the wave had spawned. Fix: only the fight's own enemies (`UWaveSubsystem::GetAliveEnemies`), the wide shot frames the main cluster (`HordeIntroRules::MainCluster`, 15 m) at the shortest distance that holds every runner (feet + head) in the real camera frustum with a 12 % margin (`FrameDistance` / `FrameWide`, pitch / FOV of the tactical camera, 15..45 m), follows the cluster while it runs (`SetCinematicDistance`), type shots prefer visible runners, the trigger waits for queued spawns (`ShouldTriggerSpawned`, grace 6 s). (4) **Heat fear through walls (all enemy types).** `AEnemyCharacter::FindNearestFireZone` / `IsInFearZone` / `FindNearestFire` require a clear line source -> enemy (`HasClearHeatLine`: visibility trace 1 m above the higher end, pawns pass, `EnemyAIRules::FeelsHeat`). (5) **Turn-based grid inside the base building.** Cause: the grid was always centred on the leader; the flatness check (2026-09-30) compared only the squad's feet with the median ground; `BakeObstacles` counted ANY overlap with the WorldStatic channel, so the yard's tutorial trigger volume made every yard cell an obstacle while the building's flat floor stayed walkable. Fix: `TurnArenaRules` + `UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem::FindTurnArena` sample the cells round the squad (ground, roof overhead 4.5 m, anything blocking pawns except the squad / enemies / barrels / barricades / turrets / mines / wires, navmesh) and pick the nearest grid whose middle 8 x 8 cells are open, level (30 cm) navigable ground holding the squad (a wall / building on the outer 3-cell ring becomes obstacle cells); none -> the Space-hold entry is refused with the reason ("only in the open - not inside or under a building", "walls, vehicles or buildings in the way", "level ground"); a direct entry (smokes) without an arena keeps the old leader-centred grid; `BakeObstacles` counts blocking overlaps only; `Codex.TurnArena.Debug 1` logs the cell map. (6) **Back to the enemy after a retreat (user).** `ThreatFacingRules`: standing in a real-time fight with no target in his sights, no facing of his own (cover, group-cover / warm-spot facing, `SetFacingPoint`, quick fire) and not on the grid, an operative turns at his stance turn rate to the enemy he last fought (where he last saw it), else the nearest live enemy within 30 m; the fire posture then picks targets as usual. Tests: `CodexTactics.Squad.Orders.PostFightHold`, `Combat.HordeIntro.Framing.WaveClusterInTheFrustum`, `Combat.HordeIntro.Trigger.WaitsForTheSpawn`, `Characters.EnemyAI.HeatFearNeedsClearLine`, `Tactics.TurnArena.*` (3), `Squad.ThreatFacing.TurnsBackAfterTheRetreat`; 615 unit tests. New smokes `BarricadeVaultBackSmoke`, `HeatFearWallSmoke`, `TurnArenaLakeSmoke` (lake), `RetreatFacingSmoke`; `HordeIntroSmoke` (a parked level hound 60 m away, frustum / visibility / screen-size checks per shot), `VictorySmoke` (the squad stays where the fight ended) updated. Verified: build -FullUnity, find_unity_clashes 0, all unit tests, 31 smokes PASS (the four new, HordeIntro, Victory, Tutorial, TutorialAmbushLake x4, LakeQuest, CombatFlow, WaveCombat, Vault, Relocation, PauseBarrelOrder, TacticalHeat, Barrel, SaveRoundTrip, Facing, Posture, Cover, Movement, EnemyAI, Mission, LevelWave, Deployable, TurnBased, TurnWalk, TurnBasedBarricade, TurnBasedPush, TurnRules, QuickFire). |

| (this, agents/work) | **fix(interactables): a disarm order from the cover leaves the wall; an action window closes when its object is gone / disarmed / spent (game designer 2026-10-21).** (1) "In cover, pressed to the wall, I order a disarm and he does nothing": a wall-hugger is usually inside the disarm reach (`DisarmStandOffCm` 60 + tolerance 25 cm of the wire line), so `RequestInteraction` opened the menu at once and never called `OrderMoveTo` - the only place that leaves the cover; Disarm then ran in cover (crouch / facing overridden by the cover pose, interaction clips are off in cover). Now `UInteractionSubsystem::StepOffCoverForDisarm` (real time, disarm orders = wire / mine / rigged object, never for what `OrderMoveTo` refuses: rage / panic / knocked down / aboard) takes him off the wall before the menu, and `ConfirmActionMenu` does the same for a menu opened in cover by another path (the pause, `OpenMenuNow`); farther away `OrderMoveTo` already left the cover. `SetFacingPoint` now clears a stale parked-follower idle facing (it turned him back right after the menu opened). (2) The window of a wire clicked again while he disarms stayed on screen (and Cancel could not close it): `CloseMenu` was keyed by the weak `MenuTarget`, stale once the wire is destroyed. Now `bMenuOpen` keys it, and `UInteractionSubsystem::Tick` -> `CloseStaleWindows` closes the window when its object is destroyed, `AInteractableActor::ShouldCloseActionMenu()` (new virtual: awaiting the pickup grab; tripwire: tripped; mine: detonated), or its `bTrapped` changed (defused / spent crate, barrel); a loot dialog whose crate was destroyed closes too. `IsActionMenuOpen()` = `bMenuOpen && target alive`. Smokes `DisarmCoverSmoke` (new: wall + PMC wires; click from the cover in / out of reach, Confirm in cover, second click while disarming, destroyed wire + Cancel, balanced open / close broadcasts via `UEventBusProbe::ListenActionMenu`), `LowCoverMoveSmoke` / `LowCoverMoveLakeSmoke` (new, see demo01-lake.md: the reported "stuck in low cover" NOT reproduced). |

| (this, agents/work) | **fix(sight): the squad's vision cones draw two tones like the enemies' - walls cut them (game designer screenshot 2026-10-20: "the obstacle isn't cut out of the operatives' vision cones").** Root cause: the operative cone drew ONE level, a standing enemy's 150 cm profile; a line from his eye (160 standing / 95 crouched) to a 150 cm point clears every wall lower than both, so a 0.8-1.2 m barricade never cast a shadow (consistent, but it hid the useful part: a crouched enemy behind it is NOT seen). Now `VisionConeVisibility::MakeOperativeSightSpec` = the squad's sight rule (`CanOperativeSee`: his stance / peek eye, FOV, the trace) at two enemy profiles: **full** = he would see even a crouched enemy (90 cm) there, **light** = only a standing one (150 cm), **cut** = nobody. Honest consequences: a wall below 90 cm hides nothing from a crouched or standing operative (the cone stays whole over a 0.8 m wall); a 1.2 m wall above a crouched man's 95 cm eyes cuts everything behind it; from 5 m back (160 cm eyes) it leaves a light strip ~3 m deep; a 3 m wall cuts. Console `Codex.VisionCones.SquadLevels` 2 (1 = the old single level, A/B). Cost (VisionConeSmoke, 3 operative cones, walls): 121 -> 131 rays, 376 -> 433 traces, 0.58 -> 0.68 ms ray work per rebuild on the task threads, game thread 0.265 -> 0.286 ms per rebuild; throttling unchanged. Tests `CodexTactics.Combat.VisionCone.Occlusion.OperativeLevels` (563 unit tests green, `-FullUnity` ok, unity clashes 0); `VisionConeSmoke` stages 40-47 (crouched behind 0.8 m / 1.2 m walls + standing 5 m back + 3 m wall: drawn level = `CanOperativeSee` in regions and on a 75 cm grid, 0 mismatches; `Shot` -> `Saved/Screenshots/squad_cones_occluded.png`); VisionCone, Sight, PeekSight, LeanPose, GroupCover, Minimap, Tutorial PASS. | (this, agents/work) | **Bug hunt 2026-10-18 - the commander at a corner no longer stands 40 s beside an enemy without a shot; planned moves no longer vanish in a melee scrum.** Repro: `CornerHoldSmoke Seed=N` (new arg; reseeds the rolls) failed 2 of 6 / 2 of 9 seeds ("flank rush: 2 of 5 down"), `HordeIntroSmoke` ~1 in 5 ("the planned move runs (moving 0, 500 of 500 cm left)"). New dev traces: `Codex.Debug.FireTrace 1` (`[FireTrace]`: every real-time fire decision on a reason change + every 2 s while an enemy is within 5 m and no shot leaves: reason - cease fire / reloading / knocked down / no target / open stance turning / corner hold cone / cooldown - and the 3 nearest enemies' shot-line verdicts: not visible / dead zone / out of range / line blocked / ok, residual; `EvaluateShotLine(..., OutReason)`, `RunCombatShooting`), `Codex.Debug.MoveTrace 1` (`[MoveTrace]`: path per move order, who drops a running order (StopOperative + call stack), move completions with distance / velocity). **Root cause 1 (CornerHold):** in the flank phase the frostbitten (and hounds) stop 1.65-1.75 m off - outside their own bite (§9, enemy stall) - and one stopped diagonally behind the wall's corner (wall frame F 476 / R -44 vs. him at F 330 / R 50): hidden (the wall between his eye and it), line blocked from the open stance; the open-shot return kept him off the wall because any enemy within `FlankRushBreakCm` 7 m (it, and two spitters 6.8 m off behind the wall) pinned him - he stood open with no target until the 45 s cap. Fix (GAME): (a) `CoverFacingRules::ClassifyOpenShotThreat` / `OpenShotThreatsKeepHimOut` in `UpdateOpenShotReturn`: an enemy waiting at his side round the corner (<= 3 m, covered, not hittable from out here, < 100 cm/s) sends him back to the corner hold unless another one pins him (hittable / uncovered / on the move); spitters waiting farther off behind the wall alone still keep him out as before; (b) `SightRules::SquadTouchRevealCm` 200: an enemy within 2 m of an operative is seen without a line of sight, so the corner hold (lean-out shot line) can fire at it. Evidence after: the frostbitten is shot from the corner 2-3 s after the return. **Root cause 2 (HordeIntro):** the planned 5 m move got a valid 2-point path and finished at once as `Success` with 0 cm moved: Detour crowd's overshoot test ("moved too far": crowd move direction . to-goal < 0) is armed from the first update of a single-segment path; with a hound standing on her path / a stale direction it ended the fresh order (a player's order silently dropped in a melee scrum). Fix (GAME): `UOperativeCrowdFollowingComponent` (operatives' path following, `AOperativeAIController` default subobject class) allows that finish only within `OperativeMovementRules::CrowdOvershootTestRangeCm` 120 cm of the path's end (`AllowsCrowdOvershootFinish`); `[MoveTrace] ... crowd overshoot finish suppressed 500 cm from the path's end` seen in 2 of 15 runs, all PASS. **Found, not shipped (user decision §7):** the enemy stall itself - `EnemyAIRules::MeleeApproachAcceptance` behind `Codex.Enemy.MeleeCloseIn` (default 0). Other situations checked: standing in the open he does fire at enemies 1.4-2 m off (the barrel residual stays under the 30 deg cone, the turn takes 1-3 frames); low cover / mid-wall cover have no corner, unaffected; turn-based has no sight / crowd path following (grid), unaffected; AUTO mode uses the same real-time code (fixed with it). Smoke changes (test only): CornerHoldSmoke flank phase also ends / passes when the remaining flankers ran off after the stall (`FlankGone`), the left-hand IK check skips hit-reaction frames (the hand leaves the rifle by design), `Seed=N`, flank positions every 2 s; HordeIntroSmoke logs the mover's state on a failure. Verified (fixed step): CornerHoldSmoke 15 / 15 + 10 / 10 on the final build + the formerly failing seeds 5 / 6 / 7 / 11 4 / 4, HordeIntroSmoke 8 / 8 + 5 / 5 + 15 / 15 (traced), `test.ps1 -Changed` (203 mapped unit tests + 53 mapped smokes, no re-run) PASS, Defend / Knockdown / Panic / Tutorial / TurnBased PASS, unit tests 481 (+4: `CodexTactics.Tactics.Cover.OpenShotReturnToCornerHold`, `CodexTactics.Combat.Sight.TouchReveal`, `CodexTactics.Movement.CrowdOvershootFinishOnlyNearTheEnd`, `CodexTactics.Characters.EnemyAI.MeleeApproach`). |

| (this, agents/coverhug) | **Fix: wall hugging broken at a wall edge in exploration (user bugs 2026-10-11: "wall hugging doesn't work again" - at the corner of a high wall the feed said "Hugging the wall, holding the corner" but the body stood off the wall in a rifle stance looking elsewhere; "at the first wall he stood strangely at the edge looking in a completely different direction, and ordered along the wall he walks backwards").** Reproduced headless by the new `CodexTactics.CoverHugSmoke` (exploration: no fight, every enemy removed; a 5 m x 3 m wall; run into its middle, then 80 cm from its left edge, shimmy away and back). Three causes: (1) **mesh turned round for good** - the 2026-10-11 bb3345c entry-yaw ease read the enter clip's weight, but `GetCoverEnterBlendWeight` is -1 once the clip is not playing and -1 counted as 0: when the auto-snap shimmy to a near corner cut the enter clip short, the full ~180 deg entry turn stayed on the mesh (measured mesh yaw -179.8 at the corner and after shimmies): the body faced the wall / round, the forward shimmy clip on a reversed body looked like walking backwards, and `GetShotAimResidualDeg` returned 180 (no shot from there). Now `CoverFacingRules::EntryHeldYawFraction` (held = 1 - weight while it plays; full hold only until the clip starts, <= 0.25 s; a clip gone early hands what is left to the timed ease-out `CoverEntryYawBlendSeconds`). (2) **corner hold with nobody around** - the 2026-10-07 corner hold stepped him into `cvr_*_fire_idle` at every exposed edge, also exploring with no threat (pelvis 90 cm off the wall plane, aiming round the corner). Now `AOperativeCharacter::WantsCornerHold` = the hold spot AND (wave combat / a turn-based fight / a fresh known threat, `CoverFacingRules::WantsCornerHold`), tunable `bCornerHoldOnlyWhenEngaged` (default on; off = the old behaviour); outside a fight the hold ends once no threat is known and no shot for `CoverEngageHoldSeconds`. Exploring at an edge he hugs with `cvr_*_look_at_idle` (the 2026-10-06 clip rule); in a fight the corner hold is unchanged (CornerHold / CornerEntry smokes PASS). (3) **clearance push at a corner** - `UpdateCoverWallClearance` measured every bone against the infinite wall plane: the corner stance's limbs past the edge (no wall there) pushed the whole mesh ~30 cm off the wall. Now only bones within the wall's span count (`CoverFacingRules::IsWithinWallSpan`, margin `CoverWallClearanceCm`; a closed side is unbounded); the no-clipping guarantee inside the span is unchanged (CoverReverseSmoke samples with the same rule). Measured after the fix (male / Female Soldier): middle and corner capsule gap 10 cm, mesh push 0, mesh yaw 0, clip `idle` / `look_at_idle`, shimmies both ways `walk_fwd_loop_*`, no bone in the wall. Tests `CodexTactics.Tactics.Cover.Facing.CornerHoldOnlyWhenEngaged / EntryHeldYawNeverSticks / ClearanceOnlyWithinWallSpan`; smokes `CoverHugSmoke`, `CoverHugMedicSmoke` (`legacy` arg = hold everywhere: FAILS at the corner) registered in `test_map.json` (rule cover). Verified: build, `test.ps1 -Changed` 51 / 51 unit tests, 21 smokes (Cover* / Corner* / CoverHug* / CommanderMode / LeftHandIK / Movement / Stance / SquadControl / ...) PASS - CoverSmoke ("... and back behind the corner", a same-frame bLeaning read) and GroupSelectSmoke failed under parallel load and PASS alone (CoverSmoke 2 / 2 alone); CoverReverseSmoke `corner` / `threat` PASS; Jev audit PASS. No asset changes. |

| (this, agents/work) | **Stance clips root the body: solo-off followers no longer slide while rising (user bug 2026-10-11).** Repro: formation -> Alt+V (squad prone) -> B (solo: followers crouch, the player may lay them down again) -> the leader scouts -> B: the followers were sent to their slots at once (`UpdateFollower` -> `FollowTo` -> `RequestMove`) while the stand-up clip played, so they slid towards the leader rising. Only `OrderMoveTo` knew about stance clips (and only `IsPlayingStanceTransition` + the sprint-from-prone case: the clip starts on the next anim update, so the first frame after `SetStance` was unguarded). **Rule (pure, `OperativeMovementRules::ComputeMoveHold / ShouldDeferMove`, `MoveHoldThreshold` 0.05 s):** a character never translates while a stance change plays: hold = max(settle time armed by `SetStance` = the ABP clip length from `GetStanceChangeDelay`, not in cover; time left of the playing stance clip). `AOperativeCharacter::GetMoveHoldSeconds()`. **Users of the hold:** `OrderMoveTo` (queues the move on `PendingMoveTimer`, replays after the hold - all player/Ctrl+click/regroup/autonomy (`AutonomousMoveTo`)/tactical-pause plan (`ExecutePlannedOrders`)/interaction/relocation orders go through it; the replay re-arms if the hold is still on), `FollowTo` (refuses while held), `USquadSubsystem::UpdateFollower` (a held follower stops if walking and repaths exactly when the hold ends - no 0.2 s lag), turn-based `UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem` grid walk (`Delay = max(prone rise delay, hold)`). Leader movement unchanged (the leader is moved by the player's `OrderMoveTo`, which already waited). **Stance after solo (unchanged by design):** `ExitSoloMode` sets every follower to the LEADER's current stance (as Godot `can_sync_stance` on solo exit), then the formation lets each keep it (user decision 2026-10-01). So prone followers + standing leader: stand up in place (prone->stand clip ~1.85 s), then walk; crouched followers + standing leader: no crouch->stand clip in the ABP (hold 0), they straighten while walking; crawling (prone) leader: followers stay prone and crawl. New `CodexTactics.SoloRejoinSmoke` (`Debug/SoloRejoinSmokeCommand.cpp`, registered + rule operative-movement-squad): 3 variants (prone/standing, crouched/standing, prone/crawling leader) - solo held positions, then after B the stance, the hold (1.85 s), no slide (< 5 cm) while held, the walk starts after the clip and the followers close >= 150 cm to the leader; verified it FAILS with the gate off. Test `CodexTactics.Squad.StanceMoveHold`. Verified: build, 11 Squad tests, SoloRejoin / Movement / Stance / SquadControl / GroupSelect / RealtimeSelect / CommanderMode / TurnWalk / Cover* / Sniper / Guard / TurnBased / Facing smokes PASS (CoverSmoke failed once under parallel load, PASS alone - known timing flake). No asset changes. |

| (this) | **Stretched Marksman (user bug 2026-10-11: elongated body walking and lying dead) - diagnosed, fix script ready, NOT applied yet (the main editor was open).** Root cause: `SK_base_mesh_ue5_1_Skeleton` (the UE5 export under the Marksman's `SKM_base_mesh_2_ue5`) has all 161 bones on the FBX-import default translation retargeting mode **Animation**, so every clip's own bone lengths replace the mesh's. The Marksman plays UE4-mannequin clips (monster pack walk / run / idles / attacks, RifleAnims, Crawl) and, since 4c75003, Manny clips (Sniper death): the UE4 clips carry `spine_02` / `spine_03` ~12 cm while this mesh has them at 0 cm (spine 33 cm -> 62 cm), legs / arms come out 8-18 % long (the Sniper death clips shrink the lower arm 24 %). Measured read-only on the 25 clips of `ABP_Enemy_Marksman` (`AnimPoseExtensions` on the mesh): torso x1.56 / legs x1.18 / arms x1.18 of the mesh's own lengths worst case; with the fix modes applied in memory all 25 clips x1.00. Operatives (80 clips incl. Manny knockdown / cover / Sniper) and Brute / Frostbitten / Cutter measured x1.000: not affected (UE4 skeletons already are Skeleton-mode + pelvis / ik Animation). Fix = `Scripts/Editor/fix_marksman_retargeting.py` (skeleton asset only; backs it up to `Saved/Backups/marksman_retarget_<ts>/`): root / ik_* / interaction / center_of_mass Animation, pelvis AnimationScaled, the other 150 bones Skeleton. Run with the editor closed (commandlet) or via Tools > Execute Python Script in the open editor; `CODEX_RETARGET_DRYRUN=1` measures in memory only. Python note: `Skeleton.bone_tree` is read-only as a whole, assign elements by index (`tree[i] = node`). Details in `skeleton_audit.md` section 6. Still open: re-run `MarksmanSmoke` + `EnemyDeathSmoke` and a rendered still after the apply; the same one-off is needed for any other Manny-exact UE5 export skeleton before a character uses it (BM2 ue5, Explorer) |

| (this) | **Cover bugs (user reports 2026-10-11, male operatives and the Female Soldier alike): wrong shimmy clip after reversing direction + body / rifle in the wall.** (1) **Reverse shimmy:** `AOperativeCharacter::OrderShimmyTo` measured the direction from `CoverSlot`, which is still the slot the RUNNING shimmy started from, not from the body; a reversing order whose target lay on the old side of that slot (he ran 3 m left, is ordered 1 m left of the slot = right of him) got `ShimmyDirection` = left: he walked right in the left clip, with an unknown threat also facing left (no turn). Now `CoverTraceRules::AlongWallDistanceFrom(Slot, ActorLocation, Target)`. Test `Tactics.Cover.ShimmyReverseDirection`; new smoke `CoverReverseSmoke` (also `threat`, `corner`, `crouch`, `medic` args: shimmy A, reverse mid-run toward a target still on A's side of the start slot, reverse from standing, ground order to the other side leaves the cover and turns; the clip names `walk_fwd_loop_L/R` / `walk_bwd_loop_L/R` must follow; fails without the fix). A ground click on the other side was already correct. (2) **Wall clearance:** root cause = the 2026-10-09 cover-entry yaw hold: after a turn > 90 deg the old yaw stayed on the mesh until the enter clip weight reached 0.5 and then dropped in ONE frame (pelvis ~1 m off the capsule pivot: a 79 cm pop; `CoverMoveSmoke` had been failing at 28dd0f7) and the held run pose blended with the enter clip swung hands / the rifle through the wall for ~0.5 s (approach along the wall, skimming it; any mesh). Fixes: the held yaw eases out WITH the enter clip weight (`UpdateCoverEntryBlend`), and a generic `UpdateCoverWallClearance`: while in cover the closest skin bone (no ik_ / VB) is kept `CoverWallClearanceCm` 6 cm outside the wall plane by pushing the mesh out along the wall normal (`CoverWallClearanceRiseCmPerSec` 300, ease-out `CoverWallClearanceInterp` 8; measured after the entry offset so the capsule's jump to the slot is not read as a body in the wall). New smokes `CoverWallSmoke` / `CoverWallMedicSmoke` (six approach angles, per-frame bone-to-wall distance; limit: inside the wall < 0.15 s) and the clearance check inside `CoverReverseSmoke`; `CoverMoveSmoke` back-pop check now `max 20 cm in a frame` + 60 cm total (the arc of the swung pose; was 25 total). `PhoneShots coverreverse` (male + Female Soldier, camera relative to the wall normal) -> `cover_reverse_fixed.mp4`. **Not reproduced headless:** the Female Soldier sinking in a steady cover pose (her bones stay >= 0.9 cm out in every approach after the fix; before it, hands / feet crossed the wall during the entry for male and female alike). Tuning note: a known threat keeps the facing fixed (walking back = the `walk_bwd` clip, no turn) by the 2026-10-06 design rule; only an unknown threat turns him with the move. |

(3) **Kneel rise between shots:** sniper one-shots / kneel clips hand over `SniperHandoffMarginSeconds` 0.05 s before their own blend-out (the FullBody slot dipped and the graph's crouch locomotion, pelvis ~57 cm, showed through; rendered pelvis now locked at 44 cm from the first shot through bolts and the magazine reload). SniperSmoke: TB drift after the shot (0.0 deg), run-to-wall body yaw step, cover entry slot covered within 0.1 s, crouch-then-fire in cover, FullBody slot min 0.99 through shot / bolt / reload. | 680ce8d | **Barrel / barricade orders in the fight (bug + user decision 2026-10-08).** User report: real-time fight, tap Space, order an operative to ignite a barrel or to move it, tap Space: nothing happened. Root causes: (1) `URelocationSubsystem::Tick` dropped every carry while `CanRelocateNow()` was false, i.e. in the real-time wave — so the push planned in the pause was dropped the frame after the release («Боевая тревога! Бросаю…»; barricades moved via their menu the same). Fix: `FRelocateTask::bPlannedInPause` (set by `HandlePauseReleased`), `RelocationRules::ShouldDropActiveTask` (a paused carry keeps running in real time; one started outside the pause is still dropped when live combat begins; turn-based drops all), per-task `DropTask`. (2) A planned Ctrl + click object shot was lost when the shooter could not fire at the release (reload after the auto-fire, misfire, frozen weapon): `AOperativeCharacter::ExecutePlannedTargetedShots` now keeps it (returns bool), `USquadSubsystem::RetryPlannedShots` retries it for `TargetedShotRules::PlannedShotRetrySeconds` (8 s; `GetPlannedShotRetry`: real time retry, pause wait, turn-based / wave end give up; feed «⏳ Оружие не готово…» / «❌ Не могу выстрелить…»). (3) **User decision: «поджечь» = with matches.** Before, a barrel could not be lit in a wave at all (Godot: a click in the pause = relocation, in real time = HQ refusal). Now a plain click on a barrel in the wave (real time or pause) opens its action menu at once (like a barricade in the pause): «Разжечь (1 спичка)», plus «Вытолкать» only where moving is allowed (`ABarrelActor::CanPushNow` asks `URelocationSubsystem::CanRelocateNow`; the pause -> the old placement flow). «Разжечь» in the fight is a use order (`UInteractionSubsystem::OrderUse`, `UseOrderRules`): real time — the operative walks up and lights it on arrival (`ExecuteAction`: a trap is defused first), pause — planned with a marker (replaces his planned move / relocation), run on the release; another move order (`OrderGroupMove`, a relocation, or his move goal changing) cancels it, 30 s timeout with a feed line, turn-based / wave end drop it. Outside the wave the menu works as before (walk up, menu on arrival). Turn-based unchanged. (4) Found by the smoke: the worker vaulted over the barricade he came to push and the push stalled; objects being relocated get the tag `VaultNavigation::RelocatingTag` (not vaultable), and a carried barricade's vault `UNavModifierComponent` no longer blocks the worker's own path (push 28 s -> 9 s). New `OperativeCharacter::GetLastMoveDestination`, `URelocationSubsystem::ClearPlannedTask`, `ExecuteRelocate(..., bPlannedInPause)`. Tests: `CodexTactics.Relocation.PausedCarrySurvivesResume`, `CodexTactics.Combat.TargetedShot.PlannedShotRetry`, `CodexTactics.Interactables.UseOrder.DispatchPerMode / .Steps`; new smoke `PauseBarrelOrderSmoke` (registered in `test_map.json`), `ClickRulesSmoke` updated for the barrel menu. No Godot reference for the fight-time barrel menu (deviation). |

After: walking 0 reversals / 27 deg/s, standing after 0, crouched corner 8 reversals / 43 deg/s, max step 5.2 deg. `Scripts/build.ps1` matches the editor by the full .uproject path (builds in the agents worktree while the user's editor has the main folder open). |

| (uncommitted) | **No tuck-back blip on arrival at a wall edge (user PIE after ba59bb4, 2026-10-07).** New `CodexTactics.CornerEntrySmoke`: every entry path (walk / run / crouched / shimmy to the edge / slot 90 + 170 cm in from the edge / stand <-> crouch at the edge; with and without a firing spitter behind the wall) traced per frame - every FullBody montage + weight, the FullBody slot weight, the cover flags; fails on a tuck-back clip (fire_to_idle, plain cover idle, look-around, the idle -> idle stance switch) after the first fire-stance clip, on a 2nd idle -> fire, on a corner-aim break on the way in, on the slot below 0.9 in the fire stance. Causes found: (1) **montage cross-fades dipped the FullBody slot to 0.44** - `PlaySlotAnimationAsDynamicMontage` stops the old montage with the NEW clip's blend-in (and an ending one-shot auto-blends out over its own 0.1 s) while the new one fades in over 0.2 s: e.g. idle_to_fire w0.12 + fire_idle w0.34 = 0.46 - the graph's plain rifle idle (the body ~70 cm back at the wall, turned) showed through at 55 %: the blip; (2) stand <-> crouch at the edge played the pack's idle -> idle switch clip (tuck back) and re-entered via idle_to_fire; (3) entering 90 cm from the edge the hold began and broke in the same instant for the snap shimmy (a later snap = step out / tuck / step out), and a shot left from the wall during the snap. Fixes (`UOperativeAnimInstance`): `PlayCoverMontage` (no engine group stop) + `FadeOutCoverMontages` (old clips fade over 2 x blend-in + 0.05 s, the slot normalises above 1), hand-off from an ending one-shot before its own blend-out (`CoverHandoffMarginSeconds`), shot clip blend-out 0.05, idle_to_fire blend-out 0.2; in the fire stance a stance switch cross-blends the fire_idle clips (`CoverFireStanceSwitchBlendSeconds` 0.35); `AOperativeCharacter`: corner hold and shots wait while `bCoverSnapPending` / the auto-snap shimmy runs; at the one exposed edge of a high wall the facing is locked to it (CornerHoldSmoke showed a turn to the closed side after an open shot). Result: slot min 1.00 on every path, sequences enter -> idle_to_fire -> fire_idle, stand -> crouch fire_idle_L -> crch_fire_idle_L. |
| (uncommitted) | **Sprint 13: drag & drop hand-over from the inventory drawer (Gemini spec, user «выполняй» 2026-10-07).** The «ПЕРЕД» dialog is retired: `UTransferDialogWidget` deleted (nothing else used it; HUD `ToggleTransferDialog` / `GetTransferDialog` gone, action bar button removed, `HudShot transfer` now shows the drawer + the new split dialog). The drawer (`UInventoryDrawerWidget`) is the single access point: new ammo lines (M16, 9 mm always; 12k / fuel / cryo / plasma only when carried, grid reflows; panel auto-height 540 wide), matches / ammo lines enabled while there is stock (click = info), a hint line. LMB on a line with personal stock is taken in `NativeOnPreviewMouseButtonDown` (`DetectDrag`); a release without a drag clicks the line (`NativeOnMouseButtonUp` -> `Activate`), a drag creates `UInventoryDragDropOperation` (`ETransferItem`, sender = leader, available; visual = purple label «Item xN»). Drop targets: a squad slot of the action bar (`UActionBarWidget::NativeOnDrop` -> `HandleTransferDropOnSlot`; the bar swallows any of our drops) and the world (`UInventoryDrawerWidget::NativeOnDragCancelled` -> `HandleWorldDrop`: absolute -> viewport pixel, `GetHitResultAtScreenPosition` ECC_Visibility; a drop back on the drawer is ignored). Both go to `ACodexTacticsHUD::HandleTransferDropOnActor(Sender, Item, Actor, Point)`: real-time order lock respected, recipient = hit operative (or owner) / nearest living mate within 2.2 m of the point, max = min(stock, recipient capacity); `TransferRules::NeedsQuantityDialog` -> `UQuantitySplitDialogWidget` (C++-built: slider + [-] [+] by the step, «ВСЁ», «ПОДТВЕРДИТЬ», «ОТМЕНА»; Esc = cancel, first in `HandleEscape`; starts at the whole stack; shows max and step), else instant. Rules (`TransferRules`): `GetItemQuantityStep` ammo 5 / items 1, `GetMinQuantity` (a remainder below 5 = the whole remainder), `QuantizeQuantity` / `StepQuantity` (the whole stack allowed off the grid), `GetRecipientCapacity` (deployables up to the carry max, others unlimited), `GetMaxTransferQuantity`, `IsWithinTransferRange` / `CanTransferTo` (horizontal <= 200 cm), `DecideRange` + `FTransferRangeContext`, `TransferQuantity` (exact quantity, partial on capacity: `Moved`, `bClampedByCapacity`). `USquadTransferSubsystem`: `ResolveDropRecipient`, `BuildRangeContext`, `RequestTransfer` (in range -> `ExecuteTransferQuantity`; far + free -> move order to 150 cm short of the recipient, hand-over on arrival; far + blocked -> feed «Слишком далеко для передачи (макс. 2 метра)»), `ExecuteTransferQuantity` (feed «Передал(а) +15 Патроны M16 бойцу …», «нет места», «(больше не поместилось)»; no event-bus event — the old transfer had none). **Blocked definition (decision):** a wave fight (`ECodexGamePhase::WaveCombat`, real time or turn-based), turn-based active, a living enemy whose current target is the sender, or the sender dead / raging / no path (the move order not accepted). In range the hand-over is allowed in every mode, also in combat, and costs no AP (as before). Pending approach (timer 0.2 s, world time): hand-over once within 2 m (sender stopped), re-aim when the recipient walks off > 1 m, cancelled (feed «Передача отменена.») when the sender follows another path (another order), stops short, either dies, or after 45 s; a new request replaces it. The legacy click mode (ring cursor, `StartTransferMode` / `HandleClick`) stays in the subsystem but nothing starts it. Tests `CodexTactics.Transfer.QuantityStep.AmmoQuantizedByFive / .AmmoRemainderBelowFive / .MedkitsQuantizedByOne`, `.DistanceCheck.RejectsBeyondTwoMeters` (also the range decisions incl. turn-based / under fire / immobile), `.PartialStackTransferDeductsCorrectly`, `.CapacityClamp` (in `TransferRulesTest.cpp`). `CodexTactics.TransferSmoke` rewritten for the drop flow (handlers called directly). IWYU fixes found by the non-unity compile: `GameFramework/Actor.h` in `SquadTransferSubsystem.h`, AnimSequence / SkeletalMeshComponent in `HudShotCommand.cpp`. **Fixes after the first host run:** a move order given in the same frame as the request was not seen as «another order» (the path baseline was captured only by the first timer tick, and during the 1 s grace the re-aim at the walking recipient overrode the player's order) — the baseline is now captured in `IssueApproach`, re-aim only on a confirmed own path; the smoke read the feed before the drop (C++ argument order) — outcome and feed are read in order and the exact texts asserted. **Additions (user, same sprint): drop to the ground and two-way crates.** Shared container `UItemStashComponent` (`Interactables/ItemStashComponent`: ETransferItem -> units, `Capacity` in units over all items, 0 = unlimited, `Add` / `Take` / `GetFreeSpace` / `SetContents`, `OnChanged`, revision; `ItemStash::ToLootItem / FromLootItem`). `ADroppedItemActor` (pile: small coloured box — ammo yellow, medicine red, food brown, engineering grey, matches orange — overhead label «Патроны M16 x15», ground trace + navmesh projection, visibility-only collision, no nav / pawn blocking; `SpawnOrMerge` merges drops within 60 cm; empty -> destroyed; click = the usual interaction walk-up (planned in the tactical pause), arrival -> `PickUpAll` with capacity clamp, leftover stays; engineering items lie as items, never armed). `ALootCrateActor` now stores its items in a `Stash` (Capacity 500): the authored `FLootContents` counts move in lazily (`SyncContentsIntoStash`, so code that fills `Contents` keeps working; bonus weapon / clothing stay in Contents), `IsLooted()` = nothing inside (derived, `bLooted` removed), a crate with stored items opens again; the loot-window click still takes the whole stack without the carry limit (Godot rule), drag-takes clamp. Generic request `FTransferRequest` (Give / DropToGround / Store / Take; `USquadTransferSubsystem::Request / Execute / GetMaxQuantity / PickUpAll`), same 2 m rule (to a crate: to its box) and walk-over / cancel logic for all four; `TransferRules::RemoveFromOperative / AddToOperative / StoreInStash / TakeFromStash`. Drop routing (`ACodexTacticsHUD::HandleTransferDropOnActor`): crate -> store, pile -> add to it, a mate's model / portrait or within 80 cm of him (was 2.2 m) -> give, anything else -> the ground; `HandleTakeDrop`, `HandleTransferRequest`, `HandleDragReleasedOverWorld` (shared world trace). Loot window: storable lines draggable (take-out onto a model / portrait / the open drawer, split dialog, capacity clamp), an inventory line dropped on the window stores it, the list refreshes on stash changes. Split dialog titles per action (`OpenForRequest`). **Decisions:** a ground drop out of reach while blocked (combat / under fire / cannot walk) goes down at the operative's feet (`DroppedAtFeet`) — getting rid of a load must always work; a store / take out of reach while blocked is refused with «Слишком далеко…». No AP cost for any of it in turn-based (the existing pickups / loot had none). Feed: «Выбросил(а) на землю: …», «Подобрал(а): …», «📦 Положил(а) в ящик: …», «📦 Взял(а) из ящика: …», «В ящике нет места», «не поместилось». **Save / load:** each crate saves `stash` (exact contents; older saves keep the authored loot), world_state `dropped_items` (location + items); load clears the session's piles and respawns the saved ones. Tests (+3): `CodexTactics.Transfer.Stash.CapacityAndTake / .StoreAndTakePartial`, `.DropToGround.RemovesExactQuantity`. TransferSmoke extended: 30 rounds dropped near -> dialog, 15 on the ground (pile label), the engineer picks them up (pile gone), 2 mines -> the nearly full engineer takes 1 (1 stays), save / load restores the pile, a far medkit drop walks then drops, far under fire -> at his feet; crate: store 10 (dialog) -> crate +10, the engineer takes 5 -> crate 5, room for 3 -> 3 stored, then «нет места». Not verified: a real mouse drag in PIE, clicking a pile in turn-based mode (same path as clicking a crate). |

Second cause found by the first run: `DuckForSafety: grenade near` at `grenade 0` — the squad's OWN grenade (in the thrower's hand before the release, then flying at the horde) counted; only enemy grenades count now (`AGrenadeActor::GetThrower`: every AGrenadeActor is an operative's today), a landed friendly one only with `bCornerAimDuckForFriendlyGrenades` (default off). (2) Wall edges bisected: `CoverTraceRules::RefineEdgeDistance` (4 steps inside the 40 cm bracket, `FCoverTraceConfig::EdgeRefineIterations`), Left/RightEdgeDistanceCm are the real edge (smoke: measured 48.8 vs real 50.0 cm) so the corner snap stands the clip side's stand-off from the TRUE edge (left corner: 29 cm for 28); `OrderShimmyTo` "already there" 30 -> 10 cm so short snaps run. (3) `IsCoverFireReady`: the sustained aim, else the fire-ready corner pose only for a threat behind the wall / round the corner (`IsCornerShotTarget(CoverThreatLocation)`), never while reloading / ducked / within the hold after an aim ended; a threat in front keeps the plain cover pose. | a3d0058 | **Cover clip sides fixed + per-side corner stand-off + corner shot only behind the wall (user decisions 2026-10-07, measured).** (1) The M4 pack names its sides FACING the wall (its enter clip starts facing it; measured from the clips' poses in actor frame by a read-only Python commandlet): pack `_L` = the operative's OWN RIGHT hand with his back to the wall (+`RightTangent`; screen-left when he faces the default camera). `CoverFacingRules::ClipIndex`: `Right -> 0 (_L)`, `Left -> 1 (_R)` (was the other way round: at a corner he stepped out INTO the wall and aimed at it, shimmy feet ran against the capsule); `ShimmyClipIndex` follows (threat on his right: shimmy right = `walk_fwd_loop_L`, left = `walk_bwd_loop_R`). Arrays stay [_L, _R]. | 6de5bd7 | **Fix (user report 2026-10-06): hidden enemies / silhouettes slid in the T-pose.** Root cause: `UTacticalSightSubsystem` hides unseen enemies with `SetActorHiddenInGame`; an unrendered skinned mesh refreshes its bones only with `AlwaysTickPoseAndRefreshBones` (`USkinnedMeshComponent::ShouldUpdateTransform`; ACharacter's default is `AlwaysTickPose`), so a hidden enemy kept a stale pose — the reference (T) pose if it was never on screen — and `AEnemyGhostActor` copied it (a heard silhouette was even rebuilt from it every 0.2 s, hence the sliding T-pose). Fix: `UTacticalSightSubsystem::KeepPoseWhileHidden` — while hidden the enemy's (and attached actors') skinned meshes get `AlwaysTickPoseAndRefreshBones`, the authored option is saved and restored when it is seen again (rendered then; off-screen enemies keep the cheaper option). `AEnemyGhostActor`: a heard silhouette ticks (TG_PostUpdateWork) — follows the enemy and copies its live pose every frame (`SyncFromSource`, the poseable copies are reused instead of rebuilt), an unheard one freezes; a new silhouette re-copies the pose on its first 2 frames (bones valid from the frame after the hide). Test `Combat.Sight.HiddenEnemiesKeepAnimating`; SightSmoke +4 checks (option while hidden / restored, heard silhouette ticks, unheard frozen) |
